63:export function tableAccessFor(table: ExpirationTrackerTable) {
64:  return {
65:    readWriteKeys(...entities: string[]): AccessCapability {
66:      return {
67:        kind: "table:read-write",
68:        description: `read/write on entities [${entities.join(", ")}]`,
69:        grant: (fn) => table.grantReadWriteData(fn),
70:      };
71:    },
72:    readKeys(...entities: string[]): AccessCapability {
73:      return {
74:        kind: "table:read",
75:        description: `read-only on entities [${entities.join(", ")}]`,
76:        grant: (fn) => table.grantReadData(fn),
77:      };
78:    },
79:    create(...entities: string[]): AccessCapability {
80:      return {
81:        kind: "table:create",
82:        description: `create-only on entities [${entities.join(", ")}]`,
83:        grant: (fn) => table.grantReadWriteData(fn), // PutItem-only IAM condition not expressible via CDK grant helpers; documented limitation
84:      };
85:    },
86:    /** GSI3 (scheduler) read-only - reserved EXCLUSIVELY for ReminderProducer
87:     * (data-model.md §3 / implementation-blueprint.md §9.2's isolation safeguard). Routed
88:     * through ExpirationTrackerTable.grantGsi3ReadTo, never table.grantReadData, so no
89:     * other function can acquire this permission via this helper. */
90:    gsi3Read(): AccessCapability {
91:      return {
92:        kind: "table:gsi3-read",
93:        description: "GSI3 (scheduler) read-only - ReminderProducer only",
94:        grant: (fn) => table.grantGsi3ReadTo(fn),
95:      };
96:    },
97:    /** GSI6 (reconciliation/retention) read-only - reserved EXCLUSIVELY for
98:     * ReminderReconciliation and OutboxSweeperReminderDispatch
99:     * (docs/architecture/m3.5-runtime-design.md). Routed through
100:     * ExpirationTrackerTable.grantGsi6ReadTo, never table.grantReadData. */
101:    gsi6Read(): AccessCapability {
102:      return {
103:        kind: "table:gsi6-read",
104:        description: "GSI6 (reconciliation) read-only - ReminderReconciliation/OutboxSweeperReminderDispatch only",
105:        grant: (fn) => table.grantGsi6ReadTo(fn),
106:      };
107:    },
108:  };

149:  "table:read-write",
150:  "table:read",
151:  "table:create",
152:  "table:gsi3-read",
153:  "table:gsi6-read",

159:export interface ScopedLambdaFunctionProps extends Omit<lambda.FunctionProps, "code" | "handler"> {
160:  access: AccessCapability[];
161:  /** Real bundling path (M3.5): TS/JS entrypoint, bundled via esbuild (see bundleEntry above)
162:   * into a CJS asset. Mutually exclusive with `code` (tests use `code` for inline synthetic
163:   * bodies; production handlers use `entry`). */
164:  entry?: string;
165:  /** Exported handler function name inside `entry`. Only "handler" is currently supported
166:   * (bundleEntry throws otherwise) - kept as an explicit prop rather than hardcoded so a
167:   * synth-time error names the mismatch instead of silently invoking the wrong export. */
168:  handlerExport?: string;
169:  /** Test-only escape hatch for inline/synthetic Lambda code. Mutually exclusive with `entry`. */
170:  code?: lambda.Code;
171:}
172:
173:/**
174: * Every Lambda in this system MUST be constructed via ScopedLambdaFunction, never
175: * `new lambda.Function` directly (implementation-blueprint.md §17.1) - it is the single
176: * enforcement point for least-privilege IAM per function.
177: */
178:export class ScopedLambdaFunction extends Construct {
179:  readonly function: lambda.Function;
180:
181:  constructor(scope: Construct, id: string, props: ScopedLambdaFunctionProps) {
182:    super(scope, id);
183:
184:    for (const capability of props.access) {
185:      if (!KNOWN_CAPABILITY_KINDS.has(capability.kind)) {
186:        // "falha synth quando o recurso solicitado não corresponde a uma capability conhecida"
187:        throw new Error(
188:          `ScopedLambdaFunction(${id}): unknown access capability kind "${capability.kind}". ` +
189:            `Known kinds: ${[...KNOWN_CAPABILITY_KINDS].join(", ")}.`,
190:        );
191:      }
192:    }
193:
194:    const { access, code, entry, handlerExport, ...rest } = props;
195:
196:    if (entry && code) {
197:      throw new Error(`ScopedLambdaFunction(${id}): "entry" and "code" are mutually exclusive.`);
198:    }
199:
200:    const resolvedCode = entry
201:      ? bundleEntry(entry, handlerExport ?? "handler")
202:      : (code ?? lambda.Code.fromInline("exports.handler = async () => ({ statusCode: 501 });"));
203:
204:    this.function = new lambda.Function(this, "Function", {
205:      timeout: Duration.seconds(10),
206:      memorySize: 256,
207:      tracing: lambda.Tracing.ACTIVE,
208:      ...rest,
209:      handler: "index.handler",
210:      code: resolvedCode,
211:    });
212:
213:    for (const capability of access) {
214:      capability.grant(this.function);
215:    }
216:  }
217:}

7:import { AppError, ValidationError, toAppError } from "../../../shared/errors/app-error.js";

11:import type { ExpirationService } from "../application/expiration-service.js";

14:export interface HttpRequest<TBody = unknown> {
15:  requestId: string;
16:  correlationId: string;
17:  claims: ValidatedClaims;
18:  pathParameters?: Record<string, string | undefined>;
19:  queryStringParameters?: Record<string, string | undefined>;
20:  headers?: Record<string, string | undefined>;
21:  body?: TBody;
22:}
23:
24:export interface HttpResponse {
25:  statusCode: number;
26:  body: Record<string, unknown>;
27:}
28:
29:export interface ExpirationHttpDeps {
30:  resolver: RequestContextResolver;
31:  expiration: ExpirationService;
32:}
33:
34:const STATUS_BY_CATEGORY: Record<string, number> = {
35:  VALIDATION: 400,
36:  AUTH: 401,
37:  AUTHORIZATION: 403,
38:  NOT_FOUND: 404,
39:  CONFLICT: 409,
40:  QUOTA_EXCEEDED: 429,
41:  DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE: 503,
42:  INTERNAL: 500,
43:};
44:
45:function toResponse(appError: AppError): HttpResponse {
46:  const status = STATUS_BY_CATEGORY[appError.category] ?? 500;
47:  return { statusCode: status, body: appError.toJSON() };
48:}
49:
50:async function withErrorMapping(fn: () => Promise<HttpResponse>): Promise<HttpResponse> {
51:  try {
52:    return await fn();
53:  } catch (err) {
54:    const appError =
55:      err instanceof AuthorizationDeniedError
56:        ? new AuthorizationError(err.message, { reason: err.reason })
57:        : err instanceof AppError
58:          ? err
59:          : toAppError(err);
60:    return toResponse(appError);
61:  }
62:}
63:
64:function requireItemId(req: HttpRequest): string {
65:  const itemId = req.pathParameters?.["itemId"];
66:  if (!itemId) {
67:    throw new ValidationError("Missing itemId path parameter.");
68:  }
69:  return itemId;
70:}
71:
72:function requireExpectedVersion(req: HttpRequest): number {
73:  const raw = req.headers?.["if-match"] ?? req.queryStringParameters?.["expectedVersion"];
74:  const version = Number(raw);
75:  if (!raw || Number.isNaN(version)) {
76:    throw new ValidationError("Missing or invalid expected version (If-Match header).");
77:  }
78:  return version;
79:}
80:
81:export async function handleCreateItem(deps: ExpirationHttpDeps, req: HttpRequest<CreateItemInput>): Promise<HttpResponse> {
82:  return withErrorMapping(async () => {
83:    if (!req.body) throw new ValidationError("Missing request body.");
84:    const context = await deps.resolver.resolve({ claims: req.claims, requestId: req.requestId, correlationId: req.correlationId });
85:    const item = await deps.expiration.createItem(context, req.body);
86:    return { statusCode: 201, body: { item } };
87:  });
88:}
89:
90:export async function handleGetItem(deps: ExpirationHttpDeps, req: HttpRequest): Promise<HttpResponse> {
91:  return withErrorMapping(async () => {
92:    const itemId = requireItemId(req);
93:    const context = await deps.resolver.resolve({ claims: req.claims, requestId: req.requestId, correlationId: req.correlationId });
94:    const item = await deps.expiration.getItem(context, itemId);
95:    return { statusCode: 200, body: { item } };
96:  });
97:}

100:  return withErrorMapping(async () => {
101:    const itemId = requireItemId(req);
102:    if (!req.body) throw new ValidationError("Missing request body.");
103:    const expectedVersion = requireExpectedVersion(req);
104:    const context = await deps.resolver.resolve({ claims: req.claims, requestId: req.requestId, correlationId: req.correlationId });
105:    const item = await deps.expiration.updateItem(context, itemId, req.body, expectedVersion);
106:    return { statusCode: 200, body: { item } };
107:  });
108:}

52:/** Unicode combining-diacritical-marks block (U+0300-U+036F), left behind by NFD decomposition (e.g. "é" -> "e" + U+0301). Built from code points rather than a literal character class to avoid any ambiguity from non-ASCII source bytes. */

1:/**
2: * Single-table DynamoDB construct — data-model.md §3 (GSI1-GSI6) + §"Governança do
3: * single-table", on-demand billing per decisions-log.md D-014, PITR per
4: * implementation-blueprint.md §17.2.
5: *
6: * GSI3 is the documented exception: its PK (GSI3PK) is deliberately NOT prefixed with
7: * TENANT#tenantId (data-model.md §3 "única exceção... IAM do índice restrito
8: * exclusivamente ao ReminderProducer"). This construct exposes a narrow
9: * `grantGsi3ReadTo` method instead of a general grantReadData-on-GSI3 path, so callers
10: * cannot accidentally give a tenant-facing function access to it.
11: *
12: * M3.5: GSI6 gains the same treatment for two specific global (non-tenant-prefixed) key
13: * families - `WORKSTATE#CLAIMED`/`WORKSTATE#DST_PENDING` (reminder reconciliation
14: * candidates) and `RECON#OUTBOX#PENDING` (outbox sweeper) - alongside its existing
15: * tenant-scoped uses (retention/purge, upload slots, per docs/architecture/data-model.md
16: * §"GSI6"). `docs/architecture/m3.5-runtime-design.md` fixes exactly two roles allowed to
17: * query it: `ReminderReconciliation` and `OutboxSweeperReminderDispatch`. GSI6 is therefore
18: * EXCLUDED from `tenantFacingResources()`/`grantReadWriteData`/`grantReadData` (same
19: * treatment as GSI3) and exposed only via `grantGsi6ReadTo`.
20: */
21:import { RemovalPolicy } from "aws-cdk-lib";
22:import * as dynamodb from "aws-cdk-lib/aws-dynamodb";
23:import * as iam from "aws-cdk-lib/aws-iam";

25:import type { IGrantable } from "aws-cdk-lib/aws-iam";
26:
27:export interface ExpirationTrackerTableProps {
28:  /** RETAIN in real environments; DESTROY only for ephemeral/test stacks. */
29:  removalPolicy?: RemovalPolicy;
30:}
31:
32:export class ExpirationTrackerTable extends Construct {
33:  readonly table: dynamodb.Table;
34:
35:  constructor(scope: Construct, id: string, props: ExpirationTrackerTableProps = {}) {
36:    super(scope, id);
37:
38:    this.table = new dynamodb.Table(this, "Table", {
39:      partitionKey: { name: "PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
40:      sortKey: { name: "SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
41:      billingMode: dynamodb.BillingMode.PAY_PER_REQUEST, // D-014: on-demand
42:      pointInTimeRecoverySpecification: { pointInTimeRecoveryEnabled: true }, // §17.2
43:      encryption: dynamodb.TableEncryption.AWS_MANAGED, // CMK upgrade tracked as infra follow-up, see report
44:      removalPolicy: props.removalPolicy ?? RemovalPolicy.RETAIN,
45:      timeToLiveAttribute: "purgeAfterTtl", // auxiliary cleanup only, never operational trigger (data-model.md §3)
46:      // M3.5: NEW_IMAGE stream feeds DispatchOutboxRelay (docs/architecture/
47:      // m3.5-runtime-design.md "Decisão central: outbox durável") - it only needs the
48:      // post-write item shape, never the old image.
49:      stream: dynamodb.StreamViewType.NEW_IMAGE,
50:    });
51:
52:    // GSI1 - vencimentos/dashboard: PK=TENANT#t#ITEMSTATUS#<status>, SK=DUE#<dueDate>#ITEM#i
53:    this.table.addGlobalSecondaryIndex({
54:      indexName: "GSI1",
55:      partitionKey: { name: "GSI1PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
56:      sortKey: { name: "GSI1SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
57:    });
58:
59:    // GSI2 - responsável/categoria
60:    this.table.addGlobalSecondaryIndex({
61:      indexName: "GSI2",
62:      partitionKey: { name: "GSI2PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
63:      sortKey: { name: "GSI2SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
64:    });
65:
66:    // GSI3 - scheduler. Global PK (no tenantId), IAM-restricted to ReminderProducer only
67:    // (M2+ deliverable) - grantGsi3ReadTo below is the only exposed access path.
68:    this.table.addGlobalSecondaryIndex({
69:      indexName: "GSI3",
70:      partitionKey: { name: "GSI3PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
71:      sortKey: { name: "GSI3SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
72:      projectionType: dynamodb.ProjectionType.KEYS_ONLY, // minimal projection, no business content (data-model.md §3)
73:    });
74:
75:    // GSI4 - membership por usuário: PK=TENANT#t#USER#u, SK=ORG#o#MEMBERSHIP#m
76:    this.table.addGlobalSecondaryIndex({
77:      indexName: "GSI4",
78:      partitionKey: { name: "GSI4PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
79:      sortKey: { name: "GSI4SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
80:    });
81:
82:    // GSI5 - provider callback
83:    this.table.addGlobalSecondaryIndex({
84:      indexName: "GSI5",
85:      partitionKey: { name: "GSI5PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
86:      sortKey: { name: "GSI5SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
87:    });
88:
89:    // GSI6 - retenção/reconciliação
90:    this.table.addGlobalSecondaryIndex({
91:      indexName: "GSI6",
92:      partitionKey: { name: "GSI6PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
93:      sortKey: { name: "GSI6SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
94:    });
95:  }
96:
97:  /**
98:   * Resource ARNs for every GSI EXCEPT GSI3 (data-model.md §3's isolation safeguard).
99:   *
100:   * M3 correction: CDK's built-in `Table.grantReadWriteData`/`grantReadData`/`grant()`
101:   * helpers always include a blanket `<tableArn>/index/*` resource whenever the table has
102:   * ANY index - there is no way to exclude a single GSI through them. Using those helpers
103:   * for tenant-facing functions would silently grant `dynamodb:Query` on GSI3 to every
104:   * such function, contradicting the documented safeguard ("IAM do índice restrito
105:   * exclusivamente ao ReminderProducer"). This construct therefore builds its own
106:   * PolicyStatements with an explicit resource list (base table + the 5 non-GSI3 indexes)
107:   * instead of delegating to those helpers.
108:   */
109:  private tenantFacingResources(): string[] {
110:    const indexNames = ["GSI1", "GSI2", "GSI4", "GSI5"];
111:    return [
112:      this.table.tableArn,
113:      ...indexNames.map((name) => `${this.table.tableArn}/index/${name}`),
114:    ];
115:  }
116:
117:  private gsi3Resource(): string {
118:    return `${this.table.tableArn}/index/GSI3`;
119:  }
120:
121:  private gsi6Resource(): string {
122:    return `${this.table.tableArn}/index/GSI6`;
123:  }
124:
125:  /** General read/write grant on the base table + GSI1/GSI2/GSI4/GSI5 (tenant-scoped indexes). Never includes GSI3/GSI6 - see tenantFacingResources() above. */
126:  grantReadWriteData(grantee: IGrantable) {
127:    grantee.grantPrincipal.addToPrincipalPolicy(
128:      new iam.PolicyStatement({
129:        actions: [
130:          "dynamodb:BatchGetItem",
131:          "dynamodb:GetItem",
132:          "dynamodb:Query",
133:          "dynamodb:Scan",
134:          "dynamodb:BatchWriteItem",
135:          "dynamodb:PutItem",
136:          "dynamodb:UpdateItem",
137:          "dynamodb:DeleteItem",
138:          "dynamodb:ConditionCheckItem",
139:          "dynamodb:DescribeTable",
140:        ],
141:        resources: this.tenantFacingResources(),
142:      }),
143:    );
144:    if (this.table.encryptionKey) {
145:      this.table.encryptionKey.grantEncryptDecrypt(grantee);
146:    }
147:  }
148:
149:  grantReadData(grantee: IGrantable) {
150:    grantee.grantPrincipal.addToPrincipalPolicy(
151:      new iam.PolicyStatement({
152:        actions: ["dynamodb:BatchGetItem", "dynamodb:GetItem", "dynamodb:Query", "dynamodb:Scan", "dynamodb:DescribeTable"],
153:        resources: this.tenantFacingResources(),
154:      }),
155:    );
156:    if (this.table.encryptionKey) {
157:      this.table.encryptionKey.grantDecrypt(grantee);
158:    }
159:  }
160:
161:  /** The ONLY sanctioned way to read GSI3 - resource is the GSI3 index ARN exclusively (never the table or any other index), intended solely for the M3 ReminderProducer. test/infra/stack.test.ts's isolation test asserts no other function's synthesized IAM policy references this index. */
162:  grantGsi3ReadTo(grantee: IGrantable) {
163:    grantee.grantPrincipal.addToPrincipalPolicy(
164:      new iam.PolicyStatement({
165:        actions: ["dynamodb:Query", "dynamodb:GetItem"],
166:        resources: [this.gsi3Resource()],
167:      }),
168:    );
169:    if (this.table.encryptionKey) {
170:      this.table.encryptionKey.grantDecrypt(grantee);
171:    }
172:  }
173:
174:  /** The ONLY sanctioned way to read GSI6 - resource is the GSI6 index ARN exclusively.
175:   * `docs/architecture/m3.5-runtime-design.md` fixes exactly two callers:
176:   * `ReminderReconciliation` (WORKSTATE#CLAIMED/WORKSTATE#DST_PENDING candidates) and
177:   * `OutboxSweeperReminderDispatch` (RECON#OUTBOX#PENDING). test/infra/stack.test.ts's
178:   * isolation test asserts no other function's synthesized IAM policy references this index. */
179:  grantGsi6ReadTo(grantee: IGrantable) {
180:    grantee.grantPrincipal.addToPrincipalPolicy(
181:      new iam.PolicyStatement({
182:        actions: ["dynamodb:Query", "dynamodb:GetItem"],
183:        resources: [this.gsi6Resource()],
184:      }),
185:    );
186:    if (this.table.encryptionKey) {
187:      this.table.encryptionKey.grantDecrypt(grantee);
188:    }
189:  }
190:}

4: * dispatch's own claim-by-occurrenceId already provides it, FIFO would add throughput
5: * limits without removing that need), maxReceiveCount=5 per implementation-blueprint.md

10:import * as cloudwatch from "aws-cdk-lib/aws-cloudwatch";
11:import { Construct } from "constructs";
12:
13:export interface ReminderDispatchQueueProps {
14:  /** Must be >= 6x this value (visibility timeout sizing rule). Defaults to the
15:   * ReminderDispatch Lambda's own default timeout (10s, ScopedLambdaFunction). */
16:  consumerTimeoutSeconds?: number;
17:}

19:export class ReminderDispatchQueue extends Construct {

27:      retentionPeriod: Duration.days(14),
28:      encryption: sqs.QueueEncryption.SQS_MANAGED,
29:    });
30:
31:    const consumerTimeout = props.consumerTimeoutSeconds ?? 10;
32:    this.queue = new sqs.Queue(this, "Queue", {
33:      retentionPeriod: Duration.days(4),
34:      visibilityTimeout: Duration.seconds(consumerTimeout * 6),
35:      receiveMessageWaitTime: Duration.seconds(20), // long polling
36:      encryption: sqs.QueueEncryption.SQS_MANAGED,
37:      deadLetterQueue: { queue: this.deadLetterQueue, maxReceiveCount: 5 },
38:    });

40:    // implementation-blueprint.md line 879: DLQ age alarm at 1h/4h.
41:    new cloudwatch.Alarm(this, "DlqAgeAlarm", {
42:      metric: this.deadLetterQueue.metricApproximateAgeOfOldestMessage({ period: Duration.minutes(5) }),
43:      threshold: Duration.hours(1).toSeconds(),
44:      evaluationPeriods: 1,
45:      comparisonOperator: cloudwatch.ComparisonOperator.GREATER_THAN_THRESHOLD,

11:import { UserRepository } from "../../src/modules/identity/persistence/user-repository.js";

13:import { TenantQuotaService } from "../../src/modules/identity/application/quota.js";
14:import { authorize, AuthorizationDeniedError } from "../../src/modules/identity/domain/authorization.js";

18:function claims(sub: string, overrides: Partial<ValidatedClaims> = {}): ValidatedClaims {
19:  return {
20:    sub,
21:    tokenId: `jti-${sub}`,
22:    issuedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
23:    expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000).toISOString(),
24:    ...overrides,
25:  };
26:}
27:
28:describe("Cross-tenant isolation (negative suite)", () => {
29:  let store: InMemoryIdentityStore;
30:  let resolver: RequestContextResolver;
31:  let quota: TenantQuotaService;
32:  let users: UserRepository;
33:
34:  beforeEach(() => {
35:    store = new InMemoryIdentityStore();
36:    const mappings = new IdentityMappingRepository(store);
37:    users = new UserRepository(store);
38:    resolver = new RequestContextResolver(mappings, users, makeIdGenerator());
39:    quota = new TenantQuotaService(store);
40:  });
41:
42:  it("two distinct Cognito subs resolve to two distinct, non-overlapping tenants", async () => {
43:    const ctxA = await resolver.resolve({ claims: claims("sub-A"), requestId: "r1", correlationId: "c1" });
44:    const ctxB = await resolver.resolve({ claims: claims("sub-B"), requestId: "r2", correlationId: "c2" });
45:
46:    expect(ctxA.tenant.tenantId).not.toBe(ctxB.tenant.tenantId);
47:    expect(ctxA.principal.userId).not.toBe(ctxB.principal.userId);
48:  });
49:
50:  it("swapping the resource's tenantId for another tenant's ID is denied (ID substitution attack)", async () => {
51:    const ctxA = await resolver.resolve({ claims: claims("sub-A"), requestId: "r1", correlationId: "c1" });
52:    const ctxB = await resolver.resolve({ claims: claims("sub-B"), requestId: "r2", correlationId: "c2" });
53:
54:    // A tries to read a resource whose tenantId is actually B's tenant.
55:    expect(() =>
56:      authorize({ context: ctxA, action: "item:read", resource: { tenantId: ctxB.tenant.tenantId } }),
57:    ).toThrow(AuthorizationDeniedError);
58:  });
59:
60:  it("a maliciously supplied tenantId in the DTO is ignored - resource.tenantId always comes from a DB read keyed by ctx.tenant.tenantId", async () => {
61:    const ctxA = await resolver.resolve({ claims: claims("sub-A"), requestId: "r1", correlationId: "c1" });
62:

2: * Single-table DynamoDB construct — data-model.md §3 (GSI1-GSI6) + §"Governança do
3: * single-table", on-demand billing per decisions-log.md D-014, PITR per
4: * implementation-blueprint.md §17.2.
5: *
6: * GSI3 is the documented exception: its PK (GSI3PK) is deliberately NOT prefixed with

8: * exclusivamente ao ReminderProducer"). This construct exposes a narrow
9: * `grantGsi3ReadTo` method instead of a general grantReadData-on-GSI3 path, so callers
10: * cannot accidentally give a tenant-facing function access to it.
11: *

13: * families - `WORKSTATE#CLAIMED`/`WORKSTATE#DST_PENDING` (reminder reconciliation
14: * candidates) and `RECON#OUTBOX#PENDING` (outbox sweeper) - alongside its existing

34:
35:  constructor(scope: Construct, id: string, props: ExpirationTrackerTableProps = {}) {
36:    super(scope, id);
37:
38:    this.table = new dynamodb.Table(this, "Table", {
39:      partitionKey: { name: "PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
40:      sortKey: { name: "SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
41:      billingMode: dynamodb.BillingMode.PAY_PER_REQUEST, // D-014: on-demand
42:      pointInTimeRecoverySpecification: { pointInTimeRecoveryEnabled: true }, // §17.2

48:      // post-write item shape, never the old image.
49:      stream: dynamodb.StreamViewType.NEW_IMAGE,
50:    });
51:
52:    // GSI1 - vencimentos/dashboard: PK=TENANT#t#ITEMSTATUS#<status>, SK=DUE#<dueDate>#ITEM#i
53:    this.table.addGlobalSecondaryIndex({
54:      indexName: "GSI1",
55:      partitionKey: { name: "GSI1PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
56:      sortKey: { name: "GSI1SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
57:    });
58:
59:    // GSI2 - responsável/categoria
60:    this.table.addGlobalSecondaryIndex({
61:      indexName: "GSI2",
62:      partitionKey: { name: "GSI2PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
63:      sortKey: { name: "GSI2SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
64:    });
65:
66:    // GSI3 - scheduler. Global PK (no tenantId), IAM-restricted to ReminderProducer only
67:    // (M2+ deliverable) - grantGsi3ReadTo below is the only exposed access path.
68:    this.table.addGlobalSecondaryIndex({
69:      indexName: "GSI3",
70:      partitionKey: { name: "GSI3PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
71:      sortKey: { name: "GSI3SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
72:      projectionType: dynamodb.ProjectionType.KEYS_ONLY, // minimal projection, no business content (data-model.md §3)
73:    });
74:
75:    // GSI4 - membership por usuário: PK=TENANT#t#USER#u, SK=ORG#o#MEMBERSHIP#m
76:    this.table.addGlobalSecondaryIndex({
77:      indexName: "GSI4",
78:      partitionKey: { name: "GSI4PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
79:      sortKey: { name: "GSI4SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
80:    });
81:
82:    // GSI5 - provider callback
83:    this.table.addGlobalSecondaryIndex({
84:      indexName: "GSI5",
85:      partitionKey: { name: "GSI5PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
86:      sortKey: { name: "GSI5SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
87:    });
88:
89:    // GSI6 - retenção/reconciliação
90:    this.table.addGlobalSecondaryIndex({
91:      indexName: "GSI6",
92:      partitionKey: { name: "GSI6PK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
93:      sortKey: { name: "GSI6SK", type: dynamodb.AttributeType.STRING },
94:    });
95:  }
96:
97:  /**
98:   * Resource ARNs for every GSI EXCEPT GSI3 (data-model.md §3's isolation safeguard).
99:   *
100:   * M3 correction: CDK's built-in `Table.grantReadWriteData`/`grantReadData`/`grant()`
101:   * helpers always include a blanket `<tableArn>/index/*` resource whenever the table has
102:   * ANY index - there is no way to exclude a single GSI through them. Using those helpers
103:   * for tenant-facing functions would silently grant `dynamodb:Query` on GSI3 to every
104:   * such function, contradicting the documented safeguard ("IAM do índice restrito
105:   * exclusivamente ao ReminderProducer"). This construct therefore builds its own
106:   * PolicyStatements with an explicit resource list (base table + the 5 non-GSI3 indexes)
107:   * instead of delegating to those helpers.
108:   */
109:  private tenantFacingResources(): string[] {
110:    const indexNames = ["GSI1", "GSI2", "GSI4", "GSI5"];
111:    return [
112:      this.table.tableArn,
113:      ...indexNames.map((name) => `${this.table.tableArn}/index/${name}`),
114:    ];
115:  }
116:
117:  private gsi3Resource(): string {
118:    return `${this.table.tableArn}/index/GSI3`;
119:  }
120:
121:  private gsi6Resource(): string {
122:    return `${this.table.tableArn}/index/GSI6`;
123:  }
124:
125:  /** General read/write grant on the base table + GSI1/GSI2/GSI4/GSI5 (tenant-scoped indexes). Never includes GSI3/GSI6 - see tenantFacingResources() above. */
126:  grantReadWriteData(grantee: IGrantable) {
127:    grantee.grantPrincipal.addToPrincipalPolicy(
128:      new iam.PolicyStatement({
129:        actions: [
130:          "dynamodb:BatchGetItem",
131:          "dynamodb:GetItem",
132:          "dynamodb:Query",
133:          "dynamodb:Scan",
134:          "dynamodb:BatchWriteItem",
135:          "dynamodb:PutItem",
136:          "dynamodb:UpdateItem",
137:          "dynamodb:DeleteItem",
138:          "dynamodb:ConditionCheckItem",
139:          "dynamodb:DescribeTable",
140:        ],
141:        resources: this.tenantFacingResources(),
142:      }),
143:    );
144:    if (this.table.encryptionKey) {
145:      this.table.encryptionKey.grantEncryptDecrypt(grantee);
146:    }
147:  }
148:
149:  grantReadData(grantee: IGrantable) {
150:    grantee.grantPrincipal.addToPrincipalPolicy(
151:      new iam.PolicyStatement({
152:        actions: ["dynamodb:BatchGetItem", "dynamodb:GetItem", "dynamodb:Query", "dynamodb:Scan", "dynamodb:DescribeTable"],
153:        resources: this.tenantFacingResources(),
154:      }),
155:    );
156:    if (this.table.encryptionKey) {
157:      this.table.encryptionKey.grantDecrypt(grantee);
158:    }
159:  }
160:
161:  /** The ONLY sanctioned way to read GSI3 - resource is the GSI3 index ARN exclusively (never the table or any other index), intended solely for the M3 ReminderProducer. test/infra/stack.test.ts's isolation test asserts no other function's synthesized IAM policy references this index. */
162:  grantGsi3ReadTo(grantee: IGrantable) {
163:    grantee.grantPrincipal.addToPrincipalPolicy(
164:      new iam.PolicyStatement({
165:        actions: ["dynamodb:Query", "dynamodb:GetItem"],
166:        resources: [this.gsi3Resource()],
167:      }),
168:    );
169:    if (this.table.encryptionKey) {
170:      this.table.encryptionKey.grantDecrypt(grantee);
171:    }
172:  }
173:
174:  /** The ONLY sanctioned way to read GSI6 - resource is the GSI6 index ARN exclusively.
175:   * `docs/architecture/m3.5-runtime-design.md` fixes exactly two callers:
176:   * `ReminderReconciliation` (WORKSTATE#CLAIMED/WORKSTATE#DST_PENDING candidates) and
177:   * `OutboxSweeperReminderDispatch` (RECON#OUTBOX#PENDING). test/infra/stack.test.ts's
178:   * isolation test asserts no other function's synthesized IAM policy references this index. */
179:  grantGsi6ReadTo(grantee: IGrantable) {
180:    grantee.grantPrincipal.addToPrincipalPolicy(
181:      new iam.PolicyStatement({
182:        actions: ["dynamodb:Query", "dynamodb:GetItem"],
183:        resources: [this.gsi6Resource()],
184:      }),
185:    );
186:    if (this.table.encryptionKey) {
187:      this.table.encryptionKey.grantDecrypt(grantee);
188:    }
189:  }

1:/**
2: * SecureLogger - implementation-blueprint.md #14.1: "Todos os handlers usam SecureLogger.
3: * Chamadas diretas a console.* falham no lint" (enforced by .eslintrc.cjs no-console rule,
4: * scoped-out only for this file). Structured JSON to stdout (CloudWatch Logs picks it up),
5: * every field passed through the central Redactor first.
6: *
7: * tenantId may appear in structured logs for investigation (per #14.1) but must never become
8: * an EMF metric dimension. NOTE (full-audit round1/qualidade, 2026-08-19): no
9: * shared/observability/metrics.ts exists yet - this constraint is aspirational until that
10: * module is built (tracked as a real gap under Observability & Operability, not this logger's
11: * job to enforce). Do not treat this comment as evidence metrics.ts exists.
12: */
13:import { defaultRedactor, Redactor } from "./redactor.js";
14:
15:export type LogLevel = "debug" | "info" | "warn" | "error";
16:
17:export interface LogContext {
18:  correlationId?: string;
19:  tenantId?: string;
20:  requestId?: string;
21:  [key: string]: unknown;
22:}
23:
24:export interface SecureLoggerOptions {
25:  redactor?: Redactor;
26:  /** Base context merged into every log line (e.g. service name, function name). */
27:  baseContext?: LogContext;
28:  /** Injectable sink for tests; defaults to console. */
29:  sink?: (level: LogLevel, line: string) => void;
30:  now?: () => string;
31:}
32:
33:const defaultSink = (level: LogLevel, line: string): void => {
34:  // eslint-disable-next-line no-console -- this IS the sink; central logger is the sanctioned exception.
35:  const fn = level === "error" ? console.error : level === "warn" ? console.warn : console.log;
36:  fn(line);
37:};
38:
39:export class SecureLogger {
40:  private readonly redactor: Redactor;
41:  private readonly baseContext: LogContext;
42:  private readonly sink: (level: LogLevel, line: string) => void;
43:  private readonly now: () => string;
44:
45:  constructor(options: SecureLoggerOptions = {}) {
46:    this.redactor = options.redactor ?? defaultRedactor;
47:    this.baseContext = options.baseContext ?? {};
48:    this.sink = options.sink ?? defaultSink;
49:    this.now = options.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
50:  }
51:
52:  /** Returns a new logger with additional persistent context (e.g. bind correlationId once per invocation). */
53:  child(context: LogContext): SecureLogger {
54:    return new SecureLogger({
55:      redactor: this.redactor,
56:      baseContext: { ...this.baseContext, ...context },
57:      sink: this.sink,
58:      now: this.now,
59:    });
60:  }
61:
62:  debug(event: string, context: LogContext = {}): void {
63:    this.write("debug", event, context);
64:  }
65:
66:  info(event: string, context: LogContext = {}): void {
67:    this.write("info", event, context);
68:  }
69:
70:  warn(event: string, context: LogContext = {}): void {
71:    this.write("warn", event, context);
72:  }
73:
74:  error(event: string, context: LogContext = {}): void {
75:    this.write("error", event, context);
76:  }
77:
78:  private write(level: LogLevel, event: string, context: LogContext): void {
79:    const merged = { ...this.baseContext, ...context };
80:    const redacted = this.redactor.redact(merged) as Record<string, unknown>;
81:    const line = JSON.stringify({
82:      timestamp: this.now(),
83:      level,
84:      event,
85:      ...redacted,
86:    });
87:    this.sink(level, line);
88:  }
89:}
90:
91:export const logger = new SecureLogger();

1:/** Real handler for ReminderDispatch (SQS ReminderDispatchQueue), replacing the 501
2: * placeholder. Partial batch failure (m3.5-runtime-design.md §"SQS + DLQ"): only failed
3: * items are reported back, successful/duplicate/already-resolved items in the same batch
4: * are never retried. */
5:import type { SQSBatchResponse, SQSEvent } from "aws-lambda";
6:import { createDocumentClient } from "../../../shared/dynamodb/client.js";
7:import { buildReminderDispatchDeps } from "../composition/reminder.js";
8:import { dispatchOccurrence } from "../../../workers/reminder-dispatch/dispatch.js";
9:import type { DispatchCommand } from "../../../workers/reminder-producer/producer.js";
10:import { SecureLogger } from "../../../shared/observability/logger.js";
11:import { defaultSchemaRegistry } from "../../../shared/contracts/schema-validator.js";
12:
13:const client = createDocumentClient();
14:const tableName = process.env["TABLE_NAME"];
15:if (!tableName) throw new Error("TABLE_NAME env var is required.");
16:const deps = buildReminderDispatchDeps(client, tableName);
17:const logger = new SecureLogger({ baseContext: { service: "reminder-dispatch" } });
18:
19:// Contract source of truth for this queue payload: schemas/queues/reminder-dispatch.v1.json
20:// (audit round1/qualidade finding: a schema already existed but no runtime handler ever
21:// validated against it - JSON.parse(...) as DispatchCommand was a silent, unchecked cast).
22:const DISPATCH_COMMAND_SCHEMA_ID = "https://expiration-tracker/schemas/queues/reminder-dispatch.v1.json";
23:
24:export async function handler(event: SQSEvent): Promise<SQSBatchResponse> {
25:  const batchItemFailures: { itemIdentifier: string }[] = [];
26:
27:  for (const record of event.Records) {
28:    try {
29:      const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(record.body);
30:      const { valid, errors } = defaultSchemaRegistry.validate(DISPATCH_COMMAND_SCHEMA_ID, parsed);
31:      if (!valid) {
32:        // Schema-invalid payload is treated as a poison message, not a retryable failure -
33:        // it can never become valid on retry. Still reported as a batch item failure so
34:        // SQS's own maxReceiveCount+DLQ (not this handler) is what stops the retries
35:        // (m3.5-runtime-design.md §10 "Poison message").
36:        logger.error("reminder-dispatch schema-invalid payload", { messageId: record.messageId, errors });
37:        batchItemFailures.push({ itemIdentifier: record.messageId });
38:        continue;
39:      }
40:      const command = parsed as DispatchCommand;
41:      const outcome = await dispatchOccurrence(deps, command);
42:      logger.info("reminder-dispatch outcome", { messageId: record.messageId, outcome: outcome.kind });
43:    } catch (err) {
44:      logger.error("reminder-dispatch failed", { messageId: record.messageId, error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
45:      batchItemFailures.push({ itemIdentifier: record.messageId });
46:    }
47:  }
48:
49:  return { batchItemFailures };
50:}

 succeeded in 797ms:
1:/**
2: * Central redactor - implementation-blueprint.md #14.1, threat-model.md redactor gap.
3: * Loads the denylist from schemas/sensitive-fields.json (single source of truth shared
4: * with any future documentation/tooling) and scrubs field names + known value patterns
5: * before anything reaches logs, trace annotations, DLQ diagnostic metadata or operational
6: * events.
7: */
8:import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
9:import defaultSensitiveFieldsConfig from "../../../schemas/sensitive-fields.json";
10:
11:interface SensitiveFieldsConfig {
12:  redactedFieldNames: string[];
13:  redactedValuePatterns: { name: string; pattern: string }[];
14:  limits: {
15:    maxDepth: number;
16:    maxStringLength: number;
17:    maxArrayLength: number;
18:    maxObjectKeys: number;
19:  };
20:  redactedValuePlaceholder: string;
21:}
22:
23:const PLACEHOLDER_FALLBACK = "[REDACTED]";
24:const TRUNCATED_SUFFIX = "…[TRUNCATED]";
25:
26:// Statically imported (not read via fs+import.meta.url at runtime): esbuild inlines JSON
27:// imports as a JS object literal into the Lambda bundle at build time
28:// (infra/lib/scoped-lambda-function.ts's bundleEntry), which fixes two real bugs found in
29:// the M3.5 architecture review round 1: (1) `import.meta.url` is empty under the esbuild
30:// "cjs" output format, so `fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)` used to throw at module load in
31:// every real Lambda's cold start; (2) `schemas/` was never copied into the deployed bundle
32:// (`lambda.Code.fromAsset(outDir)` only contains the bundled index.js), so even a fixed
33:// path would have failed with ENOENT in AWS. Static import removes the runtime file
34:// dependency entirely - the config travels inside the bundle like any other imported module.
35:function loadConfig(explicitPath?: string): SensitiveFieldsConfig {
36:  if (explicitPath) {
37:    const raw = readFileSync(explicitPath, "utf-8");
38:    return JSON.parse(raw) as SensitiveFieldsConfig;
39:  }
40:  return defaultSensitiveFieldsConfig as SensitiveFieldsConfig;
41:}
42:
43:export interface RedactorOptions {
44:  /** Override for tests; defaults to schemas/sensitive-fields.json relative to this file. */
45:  configPath?: string;
46:}
47:
48:export class Redactor {
49:  private readonly fieldNames: Set<string>;
50:  private readonly valuePatterns: { name: string; regex: RegExp }[];
51:  private readonly limits: SensitiveFieldsConfig["limits"];
52:  private readonly placeholder: string;
53:
54:  constructor(options: RedactorOptions = {}) {
55:    const config = loadConfig(options.configPath);
56:    this.fieldNames = new Set(config.redactedFieldNames.map((f) => f.toLowerCase()));
57:    this.valuePatterns = config.redactedValuePatterns.map((p) => ({
58:      name: p.name,
59:      regex: new RegExp(p.pattern, "gi"),
60:    }));
61:    this.limits = config.limits;
62:    this.placeholder = config.redactedValuePlaceholder ?? PLACEHOLDER_FALLBACK;
63:  }
64:
65:  /** Redacts an arbitrary structured value (object/array/primitive) recursively. */
66:  redact(value: unknown): unknown {
67:    return this.redactValue(value, 0, new WeakSet());
68:  }
69:
70:  /** Redacts known value patterns (emails, bearer tokens, ...) inside a free-text string,
71:   * e.g. sanitized SDK/provider exception messages (implementation-blueprint.md #14.1). */
72:  redactString(value: string): string {
73:    let result = this.truncateString(value);
74:    for (const { regex } of this.valuePatterns) {
75:      regex.lastIndex = 0;
76:      result = result.replace(regex, this.placeholder);
77:    }
78:    return result;
79:  }
80:
81:  /** Redacts an Error, producing a plain object safe to log (never logs raw `.stack` of
82:   * SDK errors, which frequently embed request bodies/headers). */
83:  redactError(err: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
84:    if (err instanceof Error) {
85:      return {
86:        name: err.name,
87:        message: this.redactString(err.message),
88:        // Stack traces can leak values interpolated into error messages (SDKs do this a lot);
89:        // keep only the first line (type + message) to preserve some debuggability.
90:        stackSummary: this.redactString(err.stack?.split("\n")[0] ?? err.message),
91:      };
92:    }
93:    return { message: this.redactString(String(err)) };
94:  }
95:
96:  private truncateString(value: string): string {
97:    if (value.length <= this.limits.maxStringLength) {
98:      return value;
99:    }
100:    return value.slice(0, this.limits.maxStringLength) + TRUNCATED_SUFFIX;
101:  }
102:
103:  private redactValue(value: unknown, depth: number, seen: WeakSet<object>): unknown {
104:    if (depth > this.limits.maxDepth) {
105:      return "[MAX_DEPTH_EXCEEDED]";
106:    }
107:    if (value === null || value === undefined) {
108:      return value;
109:    }
110:    if (typeof value === "string") {
111:      return this.redactString(value);
112:    }
113:    if (typeof value === "number" || typeof value === "boolean") {
114:      return value;
115:    }
116:    if (value instanceof Error) {
117:      return this.redactError(value);
118:    }
119:    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
120:      if (seen.has(value)) return "[CIRCULAR]";
121:      seen.add(value);
122:      return value.slice(0, this.limits.maxArrayLength).map((v) => this.redactValue(v, depth + 1, seen));
123:    }
124:    if (typeof value === "object") {
125:      if (seen.has(value as object)) return "[CIRCULAR]";
126:      seen.add(value as object);
127:      const entries = Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>).slice(0, this.limits.maxObjectKeys);
128:      const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
129:      for (const [key, v] of entries) {
130:        if (this.fieldNames.has(key.toLowerCase())) {
131:          out[key] = this.placeholder;
132:        } else {
133:          out[key] = this.redactValue(v, depth + 1, seen);
134:        }
135:      }
136:      return out;
137:    }
138:    // functions, symbols, bigint etc - never log these verbatim
139:    return "[UNSUPPORTED_TYPE]";
140:  }
141:}
142:
143:/** Shared default instance - stateless config load, safe to reuse across a Lambda's lifecycle. */
144:export const defaultRedactor = new Redactor();

24:export async function handler(event: ReminderProducerEvent): Promise<void> {
25:  if (!event.scheduledTime) {
26:    throw new ValidationError("reminder-producer: missing scheduledTime in event payload.");
27:  }
28:  const tickMinute = new Date(event.scheduledTime);
29:  if (Number.isNaN(tickMinute.getTime())) {
30:    throw new ValidationError("reminder-producer: scheduledTime is not a valid date.", { scheduledTime: event.scheduledTime });
31:  }
32:  const result = await runProducerTick({ ...deps, shardConfig: defaultShardConfig() }, tickMinute);
33:  logger.info("reminder-producer tick complete", {
34:    scanned: result.scanned,
35:    claimed: result.claimed.length,
36:    failed: result.failed.length,
37:    minutesScanned: result.minutesScanned,
38:  });
39:  if (result.failed.length > 0) {
40:    // Non-conditional failures (not claim-race losses) - surfaced so CloudWatch alarms on
41:    // Lambda errors fire; the next tick's lookback window still covers these occurrences.
42:    throw new Error(`reminder-producer: ${result.failed.length} occurrence(s) failed to claim`);
43:  }
44:}

4:import type { DynamoDBBatchResponse, DynamoDBStreamEvent } from "aws-lambda";
5:import { unmarshall } from "@aws-sdk/util-dynamodb";
6:import { SQSClient } from "@aws-sdk/client-sqs";
7:import { createDocumentClient } from "../../../shared/dynamodb/client.js";

9:import { relayStreamRecord } from "../../../workers/dispatch-outbox-relay/relay.js";

12:
13:const client = createDocumentClient();
14:const tableName = process.env["TABLE_NAME"];
15:const queueUrl = process.env["DISPATCH_QUEUE_URL"];
16:if (!tableName) throw new Error("TABLE_NAME env var is required.");
17:if (!queueUrl) throw new Error("DISPATCH_QUEUE_URL env var is required.");
18:const deps = buildOutboxRelayDeps(client, tableName, queueUrl, new SQSClient({}));
19:const logger = new SecureLogger({ baseContext: { service: "dispatch-outbox-relay" } });
20:
21:export async function handler(event: DynamoDBStreamEvent): Promise<DynamoDBBatchResponse> {
22:  const batchItemFailures: { itemIdentifier: string }[] = [];
23:
24:  for (const record of event.Records) {
25:    if (record.eventName !== "INSERT" && record.eventName !== "MODIFY") continue;
26:    const image = record.dynamodb?.NewImage;
27:    if (!image) continue;
28:
29:    try {
30:      const item = unmarshall(image as Record<string, never>) as OutboxRecord;
31:      if (item.entityType !== "OutboxEvent") continue;
32:      const outcome = await relayStreamRecord({ ...deps, leaseOwner: `relay-${record.eventID}` }, item);
33:      logger.info("dispatch-outbox-relay outcome", { eventId: item.eventId, outcome: outcome.kind });
34:      if (outcome.kind === "FAILED") {
35:        batchItemFailures.push({ itemIdentifier: record.eventID ?? "" });
36:      }
37:    } catch (err) {
38:      logger.error("dispatch-outbox-relay failed", { eventID: record.eventID, error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
39:      batchItemFailures.push({ itemIdentifier: record.eventID ?? "" });
40:    }
41:  }
42:
43:  return { batchItemFailures };
44:}

1:/**
2: * ScopedLambdaFunction — implementation-blueprint.md §17.1. "O construct gera grants
3: * específicos e falha synth quando o recurso solicitado não corresponde a uma capability
4: * conhecida."
5: *
6: * Judgment call (blueprint is schematic, not exhaustive, about capability granularity):
7: * DynamoDB IAM cannot restrict by entity type (SK prefix) the way the blueprint's example
8: * syntax (`tableAccess.readWriteKeys("ReminderOccurrence", "ExpirationItem")`) visually
9: * suggests - IAM conditions operate on dynamodb:LeadingKeys (PK) at best, not on SK
10: * patterns. This implementation is honest about that limit: the `entities` list is kept
11: * as construct metadata (visible in `cdk synth`, useful for audit/least-privilege review)
12: * but the actual IAM grant is table-level read/write via the well-tested CDK
13: * `Table.grantReadWriteData`. Tighter per-entity IAM (via a Query/Scan condition on SK
14: * prefix, not currently exposed by CDK's grant helpers) is deferred; noted in the M1 report.
15: */

27:/**
28: * M3.5: real bundling for handler entrypoints, replacing `lambda.Code.fromInline`
29: * placeholders (docs/architecture/m3.5-runtime-design.md, "Handlers Lambda reais" /
30: * "Bundling"). Invokes esbuild directly (target node20, one bundle per function, sourcemap
31: * external, no minify - stack traces stay auditable, per the design doc) rather than
32: * aws-cdk-lib's NodejsFunction, so ScopedLambdaFunction remains the single Lambda
33: * construction point (no second construct with its own bundling path to keep in sync).
34: * AWS SDK v3 is bundled IN (not left to the Lambda runtime-provided version), per design.
35: */
36:function bundleEntry(entry: string, handlerExport: string): lambda.Code {
37:  const outDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "expiration-tracker-lambda-"));
38:  const outFile = path.join(outDir, "index.js");
39:  // CJS output (not ESM, despite the project being "type": "module") so the bundle needs
40:  // no package.json/extension gymnastics inside the Lambda zip - just index.js + index.handler.
41:  esbuild.buildSync({
42:    entryPoints: [entry],
43:    outfile: outFile,
44:    bundle: true,
45:    platform: "node",
46:    target: "node20",
47:    format: "cjs",
48:    sourcemap: "external",
49:    minify: false,
50:  });
51:  if (handlerExport !== "handler") {
52:    throw new Error(`bundleEntry: only "handler" export name is wired into index.handler; got "${handlerExport}"`);
53:  }
54:  return lambda.Code.fromAsset(outDir);
55:}

58:  readonly kind: string;
59:  readonly description: string;
60:  grant(fn: lambda.Function): void;
61:}

63:export function tableAccessFor(table: ExpirationTrackerTable) {
64:  return {
65:    readWriteKeys(...entities: string[]): AccessCapability {
66:      return {
67:        kind: "table:read-write",
68:        description: `read/write on entities [${entities.join(", ")}]`,
69:        grant: (fn) => table.grantReadWriteData(fn),
70:      };
71:    },
72:    readKeys(...entities: string[]): AccessCapability {
73:      return {
74:        kind: "table:read",
75:        description: `read-only on entities [${entities.join(", ")}]`,
76:        grant: (fn) => table.grantReadData(fn),
77:      };
78:    },
79:    create(...entities: string[]): AccessCapability {
80:      return {
81:        kind: "table:create",
82:        description: `create-only on entities [${entities.join(", ")}]`,
83:        grant: (fn) => table.grantReadWriteData(fn), // PutItem-only IAM condition not expressible via CDK grant helpers; documented limitation
84:      };
85:    },
86:    /** GSI3 (scheduler) read-only - reserved EXCLUSIVELY for ReminderProducer
87:     * (data-model.md §3 / implementation-blueprint.md §9.2's isolation safeguard). Routed
88:     * through ExpirationTrackerTable.grantGsi3ReadTo, never table.grantReadData, so no
89:     * other function can acquire this permission via this helper. */
90:    gsi3Read(): AccessCapability {
91:      return {
92:        kind: "table:gsi3-read",
93:        description: "GSI3 (scheduler) read-only - ReminderProducer only",
94:        grant: (fn) => table.grantGsi3ReadTo(fn),
95:      };
96:    },
97:    /** GSI6 (reconciliation/retention) read-only - reserved EXCLUSIVELY for
98:     * ReminderReconciliation and OutboxSweeperReminderDispatch
99:     * (docs/architecture/m3.5-runtime-design.md). Routed through
100:     * ExpirationTrackerTable.grantGsi6ReadTo, never table.grantReadData. */
101:    gsi6Read(): AccessCapability {
102:      return {
103:        kind: "table:gsi6-read",
104:        description: "GSI6 (reconciliation) read-only - ReminderReconciliation/OutboxSweeperReminderDispatch only",
105:        grant: (fn) => table.grantGsi6ReadTo(fn),
106:      };
107:    },
108:  };

148:const KNOWN_CAPABILITY_KINDS = new Set([
149:  "table:read-write",
150:  "table:read",
151:  "table:create",
152:  "table:gsi3-read",
153:  "table:gsi6-read",
154:  "queue:consume",
155:  "queue:send",
156:  "appconfig:read",

159:export interface ScopedLambdaFunctionProps extends Omit<lambda.FunctionProps, "code" | "handler"> {
160:  access: AccessCapability[];
161:  /** Real bundling path (M3.5): TS/JS entrypoint, bundled via esbuild (see bundleEntry above)
162:   * into a CJS asset. Mutually exclusive with `code` (tests use `code` for inline synthetic
163:   * bodies; production handlers use `entry`). */
164:  entry?: string;
165:  /** Exported handler function name inside `entry`. Only "handler" is currently supported
166:   * (bundleEntry throws otherwise) - kept as an explicit prop rather than hardcoded so a
167:   * synth-time error names the mismatch instead of silently invoking the wrong export. */
168:  handlerExport?: string;
169:  /** Test-only escape hatch for inline/synthetic Lambda code. Mutually exclusive with `entry`. */
170:  code?: lambda.Code;
171:}
172:
173:/**
174: * Every Lambda in this system MUST be constructed via ScopedLambdaFunction, never
175: * `new lambda.Function` directly (implementation-blueprint.md §17.1) - it is the single
176: * enforcement point for least-privilege IAM per function.
177: */
178:export class ScopedLambdaFunction extends Construct {
179:  readonly function: lambda.Function;
180:
181:  constructor(scope: Construct, id: string, props: ScopedLambdaFunctionProps) {
182:    super(scope, id);
183:
184:    for (const capability of props.access) {
185:      if (!KNOWN_CAPABILITY_KINDS.has(capability.kind)) {
186:        // "falha synth quando o recurso solicitado não corresponde a uma capability conhecida"
187:        throw new Error(
188:          `ScopedLambdaFunction(${id}): unknown access capability kind "${capability.kind}". ` +
189:            `Known kinds: ${[...KNOWN_CAPABILITY_KINDS].join(", ")}.`,
190:        );
191:      }
192:    }
193:
194:    const { access, code, entry, handlerExport, ...rest } = props;
195:
196:    if (entry && code) {
197:      throw new Error(`ScopedLambdaFunction(${id}): "entry" and "code" are mutually exclusive.`);
198:    }
199:
200:    const resolvedCode = entry
201:      ? bundleEntry(entry, handlerExport ?? "handler")
202:      : (code ?? lambda.Code.fromInline("exports.handler = async () => ({ statusCode: 501 });"));
203:
204:    this.function = new lambda.Function(this, "Function", {
205:      timeout: Duration.seconds(10),
206:      memorySize: 256,
207:      tracing: lambda.Tracing.ACTIVE,
208:      ...rest,
209:      handler: "index.handler",
210:      code: resolvedCode,
211:    });
212:
213:    for (const capability of access) {
214:      capability.grant(this.function);
215:    }
216:  }
217:}

27:
28:export interface ReminderReconciliationEvent {
29:  mode: "CLAIMS" | "DST";
30:  scheduledTime: string;
31:}
32:
33:/** Hard cap on pages drained per invocation - matches the design's "página lógica máx. 200"
34: * per page with a bounded number of pages, so a pathological backlog can't make a single
35: * invocation run indefinitely; anything beyond this is picked up by the NEXT scheduled
36: * run (every 5 min for claims, daily for DST) rather than blocking this one. */
37:const MAX_PAGES = 25;
38:
39:export async function handler(event: ReminderReconciliationEvent): Promise<void> {
40:  const mode = event.mode;
41:
42:  const expiredClaimCandidates: ReminderOccurrence[] = [];
43:  const dstCandidates: FullDstCandidate[] = [];
44:
45:  if (mode === "CLAIMS") {
46:    // GSI6 is ALL-projected - each candidate row already IS the full ReminderOccurrence
47:    // item (see ExpiredClaimCandidate's doc comment), so no extra fetch is needed.
48:    // Pagination is drained (bug found by Codex implementation review: the first version
49:    // only ever read page 1, silently leaving any backlog over one page unprocessed).
50:    let cursor: string | undefined;
51:    for (let page = 0; page < MAX_PAGES; page++) {
52:      const result = await candidateSource.listExpiredClaims({ before: now(), cursor });
53:      expiredClaimCandidates.push(...(result.items as unknown as ReminderOccurrence[]));
54:      if (!result.cursor) break;
55:      cursor = result.cursor;
56:    }
57:  }
58:
59:  if (mode === "DST") {
60:    // ReminderMaterializer (M3.5) writes a WORKSTATE#DST_PENDING GSI6 pointer whenever a
61:    // trigger's schedule was computed at an ambiguous/nonexistent local time, OR whose
62:    // timeZone observes DST at all (see reminder-materializer.ts - broadened per Codex
63:    // implementation review: a non-ambiguous 09:00 reminder in a DST-observing zone can
64:    // still need re-evaluation after an offset change, not just literally-ambiguous ones).
65:    // This query finds those candidates; occurrences in a fixed-offset timeZone never
66:    // appear here at all.
67:    const windowStart = now();
68:    const windowEnd = new Date(Date.parse(windowStart) + 7 * 24 * 60 * 60_000).toISOString();
69:    let cursor: string | undefined;
70:    for (let page = 0; page < MAX_PAGES; page++) {
71:      const result = await candidateSource.listDstCandidates({ window: { start: windowStart, end: windowEnd }, cursor });
72:      for (const light of result.items) {
73:        const policy = await store.get<ReminderPolicy>(policyKey(light.tenantId, light.policyId));
74:        if (!policy) continue;
75:        const item = await store.get<{ PK: string; SK: string; dueDate: string; version: number }>(itemKey(light.tenantId, light.itemId));
76:        if (!item) continue;
77:        dstCandidates.push({
78:          tenantId: light.tenantId,
79:          itemId: light.itemId,
80:          itemVersion: item.version,
81:          itemDueDate: item.dueDate,
82:          policy,
83:        });
84:      }
85:      if (!result.cursor) break;
86:      cursor = result.cursor;
87:    }
88:  }
89:
90:  const result = await runReconciliation({ store, tableName, now, shardConfig }, { expiredClaimCandidates, dstCandidates });
91:  logger.info("reminder-reconciliation complete", { mode, scheduledTime: event.scheduledTime, ...result });
92:}

1:/** Shared API Gateway HTTP API v2 (JWT authorizer) <-> internal HttpRequest/HttpResponse
2: * translation (M3.5). Used by every HTTP composition-root handler (TestPingHandler,
3: * ItemsHandler, RemindersHandler) so claims extraction and response shaping happen in
4: * exactly one place. */
5:import type { APIGatewayProxyEventV2WithJWTAuthorizer, APIGatewayProxyStructuredResultV2 } from "aws-lambda";
6:import { AuthenticationError } from "../../shared/errors/app-error.js";
7:import type { ValidatedClaims } from "../../modules/identity/application/resolve-request-context.js";
8:
9:export function extractClaims(event: APIGatewayProxyEventV2WithJWTAuthorizer): ValidatedClaims {
10:  const claims = event.requestContext.authorizer.jwt.claims;
11:  const sub = claims["sub"];
12:  const jti = claims["jti"];
13:  const iat = claims["iat"];
14:  const exp = claims["exp"];
15:  if (typeof sub !== "string" || typeof jti !== "string") {
16:    throw new AuthenticationError("Missing required JWT claims.");
17:  }
18:  const deviceId = typeof claims["device_id"] === "string" ? claims["device_id"] : undefined;
19:  return {
20:    sub,
21:    tokenId: jti,
22:    issuedAt: new Date(Number(iat) * 1000).toISOString(),
23:    expiresAt: new Date(Number(exp) * 1000).toISOString(),
24:    deviceId,
25:  };
26:}
27:
28:export function parseBody<T>(event: APIGatewayProxyEventV2WithJWTAuthorizer): T | undefined {
29:  if (!event.body) return undefined;
30:  const raw = event.isBase64Encoded ? Buffer.from(event.body, "base64").toString("utf-8") : event.body;
31:  return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
32:}
33:
34:export function toApiGatewayResult(response: { statusCode: number; body: Record<string, unknown> }): APIGatewayProxyStructuredResultV2 {
35:  return {
36:    statusCode: response.statusCode,
37:    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
38:    body: JSON.stringify(response.body),
39:  };
40:}

1:/** HTTP handlers for ReminderPolicy - mirrors expiration/http/item-handlers.ts's pipeline and error mapping exactly. */
2:import { AppError, ValidationError, toAppError, AuthorizationError } from "../../../shared/errors/app-error.js";
3:import { AuthorizationDeniedError } from "../../identity/domain/authorization.js";
4:import type { RequestContextResolver, ValidatedClaims } from "../../identity/application/resolve-request-context.js";
5:import type { ReminderPolicyService } from "../application/reminder-policy-service.js";

7:
8:export interface HttpRequest<TBody = unknown> {
9:  requestId: string;
10:  correlationId: string;
11:  claims: ValidatedClaims;
12:  pathParameters?: Record<string, string | undefined>;
13:  headers?: Record<string, string | undefined>;
14:  body?: TBody;
15:}
16:
17:export interface HttpResponse {
18:  statusCode: number;
19:  body: Record<string, unknown>;
20:}
21:
22:export interface ReminderHttpDeps {
23:  resolver: RequestContextResolver;
24:  policies: ReminderPolicyService;
25:}

27:const STATUS_BY_CATEGORY: Record<string, number> = {
28:  VALIDATION: 400,
29:  AUTH: 401,
30:  AUTHORIZATION: 403,
31:  NOT_FOUND: 404,
32:  CONFLICT: 409,
33:  QUOTA_EXCEEDED: 429,
34:  DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE: 503,
35:  INTERNAL: 500,
36:};
37:
38:function toResponse(appError: AppError): HttpResponse {
39:  return { statusCode: STATUS_BY_CATEGORY[appError.category] ?? 500, body: appError.toJSON() };
40:}
41:
42:async function withErrorMapping(fn: () => Promise<HttpResponse>): Promise<HttpResponse> {
43:  try {
44:    return await fn();
45:  } catch (err) {
46:    const appError =
47:      err instanceof AuthorizationDeniedError
48:        ? new AuthorizationError(err.message, { reason: err.reason })
49:        : err instanceof AppError
50:          ? err
51:          : toAppError(err);
52:    return toResponse(appError);
53:  }
54:}
55:
56:function requirePolicyId(req: HttpRequest): string {
57:  const policyId = req.pathParameters?.["policyId"];
58:  if (!policyId) throw new ValidationError("Missing policyId path parameter.");
59:  return policyId;
60:}
61:
62:function requireExpectedVersion(req: HttpRequest): number {
63:  const raw = req.headers?.["if-match"];
64:  const version = Number(raw);
65:  if (!raw || Number.isNaN(version)) throw new ValidationError("Missing or invalid expected version (If-Match header).");
66:  return version;
67:}
68:
69:export async function handleCreatePolicy(deps: ReminderHttpDeps, req: HttpRequest<PutPolicyInput>): Promise<HttpResponse> {
70:  return withErrorMapping(async () => {
71:    if (!req.body) throw new ValidationError("Missing request body.");
72:    const context = await deps.resolver.resolve({ claims: req.claims, requestId: req.requestId, correlationId: req.correlationId });
73:    const policy = await deps.policies.createPolicy(context, req.body);
74:    return { statusCode: 201, body: { policy } };
75:  });
76:}
77:
78:export async function handleGetPolicy(deps: ReminderHttpDeps, req: HttpRequest): Promise<HttpResponse> {
79:  return withErrorMapping(async () => {
80:    const policyId = requirePolicyId(req);
81:    const context = await deps.resolver.resolve({ claims: req.claims, requestId: req.requestId, correlationId: req.correlationId });
82:    const policy = await deps.policies.getPolicy(context, policyId);
83:    return { statusCode: 200, body: { policy } };
84:  });
85:}
86:
87:export async function handleUpdatePolicy(deps: ReminderHttpDeps, req: HttpRequest<PutPolicyInput>): Promise<HttpResponse> {
88:  return withErrorMapping(async () => {
89:    const policyId = requirePolicyId(req);
90:    if (!req.body) throw new ValidationError("Missing request body.");
91:    const expectedVersion = requireExpectedVersion(req);
92:    const context = await deps.resolver.resolve({ claims: req.claims, requestId: req.requestId, correlationId: req.correlationId });
93:    const policy = await deps.policies.updatePolicy(context, policyId, req.body, expectedVersion);
94:    return { statusCode: 200, body: { policy } };
95:  });
96:}

102:    const context = await deps.resolver.resolve({ claims: req.claims, requestId: req.requestId, correlationId: req.correlationId });

50:

72:      runtime: lambda.Runtime.NODEJS_24_X,
73:      entry: `${HANDLERS_DIR}/reminders-handler.ts`,
74:      environment: commonEnv,

76:    });

86:    // narrow capability routed through ExpirationTrackerTable.grantGsi3ReadTo, which

88:    // asserts this at the synthesized-template level).
89:    this.reminderProducer = new ScopedLambdaFunction(this, "ReminderProducer", {
90:      runtime: lambda.Runtime.NODEJS_24_X,
91:      entry: `${HANDLERS_DIR}/reminder-producer-handler.ts`,
92:      environment: commonEnv,
93:      reservedConcurrentExecutions: 2,
94:      access: [tableAccess.readWriteKeys("ReminderOccurrence"), tableAccess.gsi3Read()],
95:    });
96:

98:    // one TransactWriteItems - needs write access to occurrences/intents/outbox/idempotency
99:    // and read access to ExpirationItem/ReminderPolicy for the staleness check, never GSI3.
100:    this.reminderDispatch = new ScopedLambdaFunction(this, "ReminderDispatch", {
101:      runtime: lambda.Runtime.NODEJS_24_X,
102:      entry: `${HANDLERS_DIR}/reminder-dispatch-handler.ts`,
103:      environment: commonEnv,
104:      reservedConcurrentExecutions: 10,

113:      ],
114:    });
115:
116:    // M3.5: ReminderDispatchQueue + DLQ, consumed by ReminderDispatch via event source
117:    // mapping with partial batch failure (docs/architecture/m3.5-runtime-design.md §"SQS + DLQ").
118:    this.dispatchQueue = new ReminderDispatchQueue(this, "ReminderDispatchQueue", {
119:      consumerTimeoutSeconds: this.reminderDispatch.function.timeout?.toSeconds(),
120:    });
121:    queueAccessFor().consume(this.dispatchQueue.queue).grant(this.reminderDispatch.function);
122:    this.reminderDispatch.function.addEventSource(
123:      new lambdaEventSources.SqsEventSource(this.dispatchQueue.queue, {
124:        batchSize: 10,
125:        reportBatchItemFailures: true,

127:    );
128:
129:    // ReminderReconciliation (§9.5): claim-expiry revert + DST re-evaluation, driven by
130:    // `mode` in the EventBridge Scheduler input. One of EXACTLY TWO roles granted
131:    // gsi6Read() (docs/architecture/m3.5-runtime-design.md) - never GSI3.
132:    this.reminderReconciliation = new ScopedLambdaFunction(this, "ReminderReconciliation", {
133:      runtime: lambda.Runtime.NODEJS_24_X,
134:      entry: `${HANDLERS_DIR}/reminder-reconciliation-handler.ts`,
135:      environment: commonEnv,
136:      reservedConcurrentExecutions: 1,
137:      access: [
138:        tableAccess.readWriteKeys("ReminderOccurrence"),
139:        tableAccess.readKeys("ExpirationItem", "ReminderPolicy"),
140:        tableAccess.gsi6Read(),
141:      ],
142:    });
143:
144:    // M3.5: DispatchOutboxRelay - DynamoDB Streams (NEW_IMAGE) -> SQS, the durable link
145:    // between the producer's claim and the dispatch queue (§"Decisão central").
146:    this.dispatchOutboxRelay = new ScopedLambdaFunction(this, "DispatchOutboxRelay", {
147:      runtime: lambda.Runtime.NODEJS_24_X,
148:      entry: `${HANDLERS_DIR}/dispatch-outbox-relay-handler.ts`,
149:      environment: { ...commonEnv, DISPATCH_QUEUE_URL: this.dispatchQueue.queue.queueUrl },
150:      reservedConcurrentExecutions: 2,
151:      access: [tableAccess.readWriteKeys("OutboxEvent"), queueAccessFor().send(this.dispatchQueue.queue)],
152:    });
153:    if (!this.table.table.tableStreamArn) {
154:      throw new Error("ExpirationTrackerTable must have a DynamoDB Stream enabled for DispatchOutboxRelay.");
155:    }
156:    this.dispatchOutboxRelay.function.addEventSource(
157:      new lambdaEventSources.DynamoEventSource(this.table.table, {
158:        startingPosition: lambda.StartingPosition.LATEST,
159:        batchSize: 25,
160:        reportBatchItemFailures: true,
161:      }),
162:    );
163:
164:    // M3.5: OutboxSweeperReminderDispatch - the other of EXACTLY TWO roles granted
165:    // gsi6Read() (recovers publications the relay/Stream missed).
166:    this.outboxSweeper = new ScopedLambdaFunction(this, "OutboxSweeperReminderDispatch", {
167:      runtime: lambda.Runtime.NODEJS_24_X,
168:      entry: `${HANDLERS_DIR}/outbox-sweeper-handler.ts`,
169:      environment: { ...commonEnv, DISPATCH_QUEUE_URL: this.dispatchQueue.queue.queueUrl },
170:      reservedConcurrentExecutions: 2,
171:      access: [
172:        tableAccess.readWriteKeys("OutboxEvent"),
173:        tableAccess.gsi6Read(),
174:        queueAccessFor().send(this.dispatchQueue.queue),
175:      ],
176:    });
177:
178:    // M3.5: EventBridge Scheduler for the four periodic functions.
179:    new ReminderSchedule(this, "ReminderSchedule", {
180:      reminderProducer: this.reminderProducer.function,
181:      reminderReconciliation: this.reminderReconciliation.function,
182:      outboxSweeper: this.outboxSweeper.function,

 succeeded in 428ms:
1:/**
2: * ScopedLambdaFunction — implementation-blueprint.md §17.1. "O construct gera grants
3: * específicos e falha synth quando o recurso solicitado não corresponde a uma capability
4: * conhecida."
5: *
6: * Judgment call (blueprint is schematic, not exhaustive, about capability granularity):
7: * DynamoDB IAM cannot restrict by entity type (SK prefix) the way the blueprint's example
8: * syntax (`tableAccess.readWriteKeys("ReminderOccurrence", "ExpirationItem")`) visually
9: * suggests - IAM conditions operate on dynamodb:LeadingKeys (PK) at best, not on SK
10: * patterns. This implementation is honest about that limit: the `entities` list is kept
11: * as construct metadata (visible in `cdk synth`, useful for audit/least-privilege review)
12: * but the actual IAM grant is table-level read/write via the well-tested CDK
13: * `Table.grantReadWriteData`. Tighter per-entity IAM (via a Query/Scan condition on SK
14: * prefix, not currently exposed by CDK's grant helpers) is deferred; noted in the M1 report.
15: */
16:import * as lambda from "aws-cdk-lib/aws-lambda";
17:import * as iam from "aws-cdk-lib/aws-iam";
18:import * as sqs from "aws-cdk-lib/aws-sqs";
19:import { Duration } from "aws-cdk-lib";
20:import { Construct } from "constructs";
21:import * as esbuild from "esbuild";
22:import * as fs from "node:fs";
23:import * as path from "node:path";
24:import * as os from "node:os";
25:import type { ExpirationTrackerTable } from "./dynamo-table.js";
26:
27:/**
28: * M3.5: real bundling for handler entrypoints, replacing `lambda.Code.fromInline`
29: * placeholders (docs/architecture/m3.5-runtime-design.md, "Handlers Lambda reais" /
30: * "Bundling"). Invokes esbuild directly (target node20, one bundle per function, sourcemap
31: * external, no minify - stack traces stay auditable, per the design doc) rather than
32: * aws-cdk-lib's NodejsFunction, so ScopedLambdaFunction remains the single Lambda
33: * construction point (no second construct with its own bundling path to keep in sync).
34: * AWS SDK v3 is bundled IN (not left to the Lambda runtime-provided version), per design.
35: */
36:function bundleEntry(entry: string, handlerExport: string): lambda.Code {
37:  const outDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "expiration-tracker-lambda-"));
38:  const outFile = path.join(outDir, "index.js");
39:  // CJS output (not ESM, despite the project being "type": "module") so the bundle needs
40:  // no package.json/extension gymnastics inside the Lambda zip - just index.js + index.handler.
41:  esbuild.buildSync({
42:    entryPoints: [entry],
43:    outfile: outFile,
44:    bundle: true,
45:    platform: "node",
46:    target: "node20",
47:    format: "cjs",
48:    sourcemap: "external",
49:    minify: false,
50:  });
51:  if (handlerExport !== "handler") {
52:    throw new Error(`bundleEntry: only "handler" export name is wired into index.handler; got "${handlerExport}"`);
53:  }
54:  return lambda.Code.fromAsset(outDir);
55:}
56:
57:export interface AccessCapability {
58:  readonly kind: string;
59:  readonly description: string;
60:  grant(fn: lambda.Function): void;
61:}
62:
63:export function tableAccessFor(table: ExpirationTrackerTable) {
64:  return {
65:    readWriteKeys(...entities: string[]): AccessCapability {
66:      return {
67:        kind: "table:read-write",
68:        description: `read/write on entities [${entities.join(", ")}]`,
69:        grant: (fn) => table.grantReadWriteData(fn),
70:      };
71:    },
72:    readKeys(...entities: string[]): AccessCapability {
73:      return {
74:        kind: "table:read",
75:        description: `read-only on entities [${entities.join(", ")}]`,
76:        grant: (fn) => table.grantReadData(fn),
77:      };
78:    },
79:    create(...entities: string[]): AccessCapability {
80:      return {
81:        kind: "table:create",
82:        description: `create-only on entities [${entities.join(", ")}]`,
83:        grant: (fn) => table.grantReadWriteData(fn), // PutItem-only IAM condition not expressible via CDK grant helpers; documented limitation
84:      };
85:    },
86:    /** GSI3 (scheduler) read-only - reserved EXCLUSIVELY for ReminderProducer
87:     * (data-model.md §3 / implementation-blueprint.md §9.2's isolation safeguard). Routed
88:     * through ExpirationTrackerTable.grantGsi3ReadTo, never table.grantReadData, so no
89:     * other function can acquire this permission via this helper. */
90:    gsi3Read(): AccessCapability {
91:      return {
92:        kind: "table:gsi3-read",
93:        description: "GSI3 (scheduler) read-only - ReminderProducer only",
94:        grant: (fn) => table.grantGsi3ReadTo(fn),
95:      };
96:    },
97:    /** GSI6 (reconciliation/retention) read-only - reserved EXCLUSIVELY for
98:     * ReminderReconciliation and OutboxSweeperReminderDispatch
99:     * (docs/architecture/m3.5-runtime-design.md). Routed through
100:     * ExpirationTrackerTable.grantGsi6ReadTo, never table.grantReadData. */
101:    gsi6Read(): AccessCapability {
102:      return {
103:        kind: "table:gsi6-read",
104:        description: "GSI6 (reconciliation) read-only - ReminderReconciliation/OutboxSweeperReminderDispatch only",
105:        grant: (fn) => table.grantGsi6ReadTo(fn),
106:      };
107:    },
108:  };
109:}
110:
111:export function queueAccessFor() {
112:  return {
113:    consume(queue: sqs.IQueue): AccessCapability {
114:      return {
115:        kind: "queue:consume",
116:        description: `consume from ${queue.queueName}`,
117:        grant: (fn) => queue.grantConsumeMessages(fn),
118:      };
119:    },
120:    send(queue: sqs.IQueue): AccessCapability {
121:      return {
122:        kind: "queue:send",
123:        description: `send to ${queue.queueName}`,
124:        grant: (fn) => queue.grantSendMessages(fn),
125:      };
126:    },
127:  };
128:}
129:
130:export function appConfigAccessFor() {
131:  return {
132:    read(profileName: string): AccessCapability {
133:      return {
134:        kind: "appconfig:read",
135:        description: `read AppConfig profile ${profileName}`,
136:        grant: (fn) =>
137:          fn.addToRolePolicy(
138:            new iam.PolicyStatement({
139:              actions: ["appconfig:GetLatestConfiguration", "appconfig:StartConfigurationSession"],
140:              resources: ["*"], // AppConfig resource ARNs are per-deploy; scoped once the AppConfig construct exists (M1+ follow-up)
141:            }),
142:          ),
143:      };
144:    },
145:  };
146:}

148:const KNOWN_CAPABILITY_KINDS = new Set([
149:  "table:read-write",
150:  "table:read",
151:  "table:create",
152:  "table:gsi3-read",
153:  "table:gsi6-read",
154:  "queue:consume",
155:  "queue:send",
156:  "appconfig:read",
157:]);

159:export interface ScopedLambdaFunctionProps extends Omit<lambda.FunctionProps, "code" | "handler"> {
160:  access: AccessCapability[];
161:  /** Real bundling path (M3.5): TS/JS entrypoint, bundled via esbuild (see bundleEntry above)
162:   * into a CJS asset. Mutually exclusive with `code` (tests use `code` for inline synthetic
163:   * bodies; production handlers use `entry`). */
164:  entry?: string;
165:  /** Exported handler function name inside `entry`. Only "handler" is currently supported
166:   * (bundleEntry throws otherwise) - kept as an explicit prop rather than hardcoded so a
167:   * synth-time error names the mismatch instead of silently invoking the wrong export. */
168:  handlerExport?: string;
169:  /** Test-only escape hatch for inline/synthetic Lambda code. Mutually exclusive with `entry`. */
170:  code?: lambda.Code;
171:}
172:
173:/**
174: * Every Lambda in this system MUST be constructed via ScopedLambdaFunction, never
175: * `new lambda.Function` directly (implementation-blueprint.md §17.1) - it is the single
176: * enforcement point for least-privilege IAM per function.
177: */
178:export class ScopedLambdaFunction extends Construct {
179:  readonly function: lambda.Function;
180:
181:  constructor(scope: Construct, id: string, props: ScopedLambdaFunctionProps) {
182:    super(scope, id);
183:
184:    for (const capability of props.access) {
185:      if (!KNOWN_CAPABILITY_KINDS.has(capability.kind)) {
186:        // "falha synth quando o recurso solicitado não corresponde a uma capability conhecida"
187:        throw new Error(
188:          `ScopedLambdaFunction(${id}): unknown access capability kind "${capability.kind}". ` +
189:            `Known kinds: ${[...KNOWN_CAPABILITY_KINDS].join(", ")}.`,
190:        );
191:      }
192:    }
193:
194:    const { access, code, entry, handlerExport, ...rest } = props;
195:
196:    if (entry && code) {
197:      throw new Error(`ScopedLambdaFunction(${id}): "entry" and "code" are mutually exclusive.`);
198:    }
199:
200:    const resolvedCode = entry
201:      ? bundleEntry(entry, handlerExport ?? "handler")
202:      : (code ?? lambda.Code.fromInline("exports.handler = async () => ({ statusCode: 501 });"));
203:
204:    this.function = new lambda.Function(this, "Function", {
205:      timeout: Duration.seconds(10),
206:      memorySize: 256,
207:      tracing: lambda.Tracing.ACTIVE,
208:      ...rest,
209:      handler: "index.handler",
210:      code: resolvedCode,
211:    });
212:
213:    for (const capability of access) {
214:      capability.grant(this.function);
215:    }
216:  }
217:}
